   86\t\u2502                                                             \u2502\n    87\t\u2502   Query \u2500\u2500\u2192 [Retriever] \u2500\u2500\u2192 Top-K Documents                \u2502\n \u5206\u5757\u7b56\u7565\u4f18\u5316\n\n| \u7b56\u7565 | \u63cf\u8ff0 | \u6700\u4f73\u5b9e\u8df5 |\n|------|------|---------|\n| **\u8bed\u4e49\u5206\u5757** | 9.2.2 \u4e09\u5927Server Primitives\n\n| Primitive | \u529f\u80fd | RAG\u76f8\u5173\u6027 |\n|-----------|------|----------|\n| **Resources** | Server\u5b9e\u73b0\u793a\u4f8b\n\n#### 9.5.1 Python SDK\n\n```python\nfrom mcp.server import Server\nfrom mcp.types import Tool, TextContent\n\nserver = Server(\"rag-server\")\n\n@server.tool()\nasync def retrieve_documents(query: str, top_k: int = 5) -> list[TextContent]:\n    \"\"\"\u4ece\u77e5\u8bc6\u5e93\u68c0\u7d22\u76f8\u5173\u6587\u6863\"\"\"\n    results = rag_engine.retrieve(query, top_k=top_k)\n    return [TextContent(text=doc.content) for doc in results]\n\n@server.tool()\nasync def search_by_metadata(filters: dict) -> list[TextContent]:\n    \"\"\"\u6309\u5143\u6570\u636e\u8fc7\u6ee4\u68c0\u7d22\"\"\"\n    results = rag_engine.metadata_search(filters)\n    return [TextContent(text=doc.content) for doc in results]\n```\n\n#### 9.5.2 rag_mcp_server.py --port 3000\n\n# Claude Code\u914d\u7f6e\n# ~/.claude/mcp_config.json\n{\n  \"servers\": {\n    \"rag\": {\n      \"command\": \"python\",\n      \"args\": [\"rag_mcp_server.py\", \"--port\", \"3000\"]\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 9.6 MCP\u7684\u5b89\u5168\u8003\u91cf\n\n| \u5b89\u5168\u95ee\u9898 | \u8bf4\u660e | \u7f13\u89e3\u63aa\u65bd |\n|---------|------|---------|\n|
